{"lang_code":"en","pos":"det","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Whose (wallet) is this?","type":"example"},{"text":"We should buy a house. ~ With whose money?","type":"example"},{"text":"For whose benefit are you acting.?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Of whom, belonging to whom; which person's or people's."],"tags":["interrogative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"This is the man whose dog caused the accident.","type":"example"},{"text":"Venus, whose sister is Serena, won the latest championship.","type":"example"},{"text":"I dedicate this award to my parents, without whose help I wouldn't have made it this far.","type":"example"},{"text":"The victim was a youngster, both of whose eyes were missing.","type":"example"},{"text":"It is Marconi whose name is associated with the development of radio.","type":"example"},{"text":"Ammon is an Egyptian god near whose temple ammonia and ammoniac were said to come.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Of whom, belonging to whom."],"tags":["relative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"We saw several houses whose roofs were falling off.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Of which, belonging to which."],"tags":["relative"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/huːz/","tags":["UK","US"]}],"upstream_line":25208,"upstream_offset":173564296,"upstream_record_sha256":"f6d04919691d06bbe031d93a617de192f012218b4a439cbfe6c66d0a99a3d0ad","word":"whose"}

Whose have you found?","type":"example"},{"text":"He asked whose the umbrella was.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["That or those of whom or belonging to whom."],"tags":["interrogative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"This car is blocking the way, but Mr Smith, whose it is, will be here shortly.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["That or those of whom or belonging to whom."],"tags":["relative"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/huːz/","tags":["UK","US"]}],"upstream_line":25209,"upstream_offset":173597445,"upstream_record_sha256":"5be30b1d30c733b52e699d1f2b0754705463960ddc447b8801add58b818895ee","word":"whose"}
